\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q-1}dz\right)^{\theta q}\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\right)^{q-1}\,\,\,\,\text{for}\,\,\,\,\,\,\frac{q-1}{q}<\theta\leq 1,

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{s-1}dz\right)^{\theta q}\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\right)^{s-1}\,\,\,\,\text{for}\,\,\,\,\,\,\frac{s-1}{q}<\theta\leq 1,

and

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{(p-1)q}{p}}\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{p-1}.

Note that the range of
\theta
in the above two inequalities are admissible since
\theta>\frac{s-1}{p}\geq\frac{p-1}{q},

\theta>\frac{q-1}{p}\geq\frac{q-1}{q},
and
\theta>\frac{s-1}{p}\geq\frac{s-1}{q}.
Now applying the above estimates in (
3.12
) we obtain

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta q}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta q}dz+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{p-1}

(3.13)

\displaystyle+c\lambda^{(2-q)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-1}+c\lambda^{(2-s)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{s-1}.

Lastly, using (
3.10
) and the above estimate in (
3.9
), we prove
(ii)
.

Step 4.
In this step we prove
(iii)
. First, we perform an estimate similar to
Step 1.
Indeed, we estimate

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\tilde{H}(z,\nabla u)dz

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p-1}dz+\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|a(z)-\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)||\nabla u|^{q-1}dz}_{I}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|b(z)-\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)||\nabla u|^{s-1}dz}_{II}+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)|\nabla u|^{q-1}dz

\displaystyle+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)|\nabla u|^{s-1}dz.

As in
Step 1.
, we can estimate
I
and
II
as

\displaystyle I\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{p-1}{\theta p}}\,\,\,\text{and}\,\,\,\,\,II\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{p-1}{\theta p}}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left(\lambda^{2-p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{p-1}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q-1}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s-1}\right)dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p-1}dz\right)^{\theta q}+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)|\nabla u|^{q-1}dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{(p-1)q}{p}}+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)|\nabla u|^{s-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p}}+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\frac{\theta q}{q-1}}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-1}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\frac{\theta q}{s}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right)^{\frac{q(s-1)}{s}}

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p}}+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-1}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\frac{\theta q}{s}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right)^{\frac{q(s-1)}{s}}.

Therefore, applying the above inequality in (
3.11
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta q}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\right)^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p}}

\displaystyle+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-q)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\right)^{q-1}

(3.14)

\displaystyle+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-s)\frac{\theta q}{s}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)^{\frac{q(s-1)}{s}}.

Using the above estimate in (
3.9
), we get
(iii)
.

Step 5.
In this step, we prove
(iv)
. Since the proof can be deduced from
Step 3.
, here we omit the details. Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(z)^{\theta}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta s}\,dz\lesssim\lambda^{(p-s)\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta s}

and following the same steps as previous we obtain

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta s}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta s}dz+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right)^{p-1}

(3.15)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-q)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right)^{q-1}+\lambda^{(2-s)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right)^{s-1},

and hence
(iv)
.

Step 6.
In this step we provide the proof of
(v)
which is similar to the arguments in
Step 4.
Hence, we omit the details. Indeed, in this case we obtain

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta s}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta s}dz+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{s(p-1)}{p}}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\lambda^{(p-q)\frac{\theta s}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{\frac{s(q-1)}{q}}

(3.16)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right)^{s-1}.

This completes the proof.
∎

The next lemma shows that we can further estimate the right-hand sides of
(i)
,
(ii)
,
(iii)
,
(iv)
and
(v)
of
Lemma

3.1
.

Lemma 3.2
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and let the
Item

p1.
be in force. Then for any
\theta\in\left(\max\left\{\frac{s-1}{p},\frac{1}{p}\right\},1\right]
and
\varepsilon\in(0,1),
there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
such that the following estimates

(i)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta p}dz\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta p}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p},

(ii)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta q}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta q}dz+c\lambda^{(q-p)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a^{\theta}(z)|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)

\displaystyle+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-s)\frac{\theta q}{s}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)^{\frac{q(s-1)}{s}}+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta q},

(iii)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta s}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta s}dz+c\lambda^{(s-p)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b^{\theta}(z)|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right)

\displaystyle+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\lambda^{(p-q)\frac{\theta s}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s(q-1)}{q}}+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta s},

hold whenever
{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

We start with the proof of
(i)
. Let us consider the following two cases.

Case
p\geq 2
:
This case is a simple application of Hölder’s inequality and
Item

p2.
. Indeed, the second term in
(i)
of
Lemma

3.1
can be estimated as

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle=\lambda^{(2-p)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{p-2}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}dz\right)^{(p-2)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)

(3.17)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta p}\lambda^{(p-2)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right).

The third and the fourth term in
(i)
of
Lemma

3.1
can be estimated similarly. Thus we obtained

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta p}dz\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right).

Case
p<2
:
In this case, we use Young’s inequality with
\left(\frac{1}{2-p},\frac{1}{p-1}\right).
Note that, since
1<p<2,

\frac{1}{2-p}>1
and
\frac{1}{p-1}>1.
Applying Young’s inequality in the second term in
(i)
of
Lemma

3.1
, we get

(3.18)

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{p-1}\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right),

and similarly from the third and fourth term in
(i)
of
Lemma

3.1
we get

(3.19)

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{q-1}\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right),

and

(3.20)

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-s)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{s-1}\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right).

Note that here we assume that
s,q<2.
In the case of
p<2
and
s,q>2,
we can estimate the above term as previous. Combining the estimates, (
3.17
), (
3.18
), (
3.19
) and (
3.20
) we obtain
(i)
.

The proof of
(ii)
is similar.

Case
p\geq 2
:
In this case, we use Hölder’s inequality. The second term in right hand side in the expression (
3.14
) can be estimated as

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p}}

\displaystyle=\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{q(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{q}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}dz\right)^{\frac{\theta q(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-2)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right).

The third term in the right hand side of (
3.14
) can be estimated similarly as

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-q)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-1}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-q)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-2}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-q)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}dz\right)^{\theta(q-2)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-q)\theta}\lambda^{\theta p(q-2)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)=\lambda^{(q-p)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right).

Hence combining all the estimates above, in the case
p\geq 2,
we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta q}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)+c\lambda^{(q-p)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)

(3.21)

\displaystyle+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-s)\frac{\theta q}{s}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)^{\frac{q(s-1)}{s}}.

Case
p<2
:
In this case, we use Young’s inequality with
(\frac{1}{2-p},\frac{1}{p-1})
. We estimate the second term in the right hand side of (
3.13
) as

(3.22)

\displaystyle c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{p-1}\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta q}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right).

Similarly, from the third and the fourth term in the right hand side of (
3.13
), we have

(3.23)

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-q)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-1}\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta q}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)

and

(3.24)

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-s)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{s-1}\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta q}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right).

Note that here we assumed
s,q<2.
The case
p<2
and
s,q>2
can occur and in that case, we use Young’s inequality for
p<2
case and Hölder’s inequality for
s,q>2
case as previous. Thus combining the estimates (
3.21
), (
3.22
), (
3.23
) and (
3.24
) we get
(ii)
.
Next, we show
(iii)
.

Case
p\geq 2
:
As previous, the second term of (
3.16
) can be estimated as

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{s(p-1)}{p}}\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right).

On the other hand, the fourth term can be estimates as

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right)^{s-1}\leq\lambda^{(s-p)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b^{\theta}(z)|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right).

Therefore, in this case we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta s}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta s}dz+c\lambda^{(s-p)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b^{\theta}(z)|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)

(3.25)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\lambda^{(p-q)\frac{\theta s}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{\frac{s(q-1)}{q}}.

Case
p<2
:
In this case, we estimate the right hand side of (
3.15
) and get

(3.26)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta s}dz\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta s}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right).

Combining the estimates (
3.25
)-(
3.26
), we obtain
(iii)
.
This completes the proof.
∎

Now we state the energy estimate
Lemma

2.3
in terms of
p
-intrinsic cylinders. Before that let us define appropriate cutoff functions. We choose
R_{2}=\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b},

R_{1}=\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{a},

S_{2}=\lambda^{2\mu-p}\rho_{b}^{2},
and
S_{1}=\lambda^{2\mu-p}\rho_{a}^{2}
in
Lemma

2.3
. Furthermore, we have the restriction
\frac{\rho_{b}}{2}\leq\rho_{a}<\rho_{b}.

Cut-off functions for
p
-phase.
Let
0<\varrho\leq\rho_{a}<\rho_{b}\leq 4\varrho
with the restriction mentioned above and we consider the following cutoff functions:

\displaystyle\eta:=\eta(x)\in C_{c}^{\infty}(B_{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}(x_{0})),\quad\eta\equiv 1\,\,\text{on}\,B_{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{a}}(x_{0}),\quad 0\leq\eta\leq 1\,\,\,\,\,\text{and}\,\,\,\,\,\,|\nabla\eta|\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}(\rho_{b}-\rho_{a})}

\displaystyle\zeta:=\zeta(t)\in C^{\infty}_{c}(t_{0}-\lambda^{2\mu-p}(\rho_{b}-h_{0})^{2},t_{0}+\lambda^{2\mu-p}(\rho_{b}-h_{0})^{2}),\qquad\zeta\equiv 1\,\,\text{on}\,(t_{0}-\lambda^{2\mu-p}\rho_{a}^{2},t_{0}+\lambda^{2\mu-p}\rho_{a}^{2}),

\displaystyle 0\leq\zeta\leq 1\,\,\,\text{and}\,\,\,|\partial_{t}\zeta|\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda^{2\mu-p}(\rho_{b}-\rho_{a})^{2}}.

Then the energy estimate for
p
-phase reads as:

Lemma 3.3
.

Let
u
be a weak solution of (
1.1
). Then we have the following energy estimate:

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{a}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{a}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{a}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{a}}\right|^{2}dx+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{a}}(z_{0})}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)\,dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}(\rho_{b}-\rho_{a})}\right|\right)\,dz+\lambda^{p-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}(\rho_{b}-\rho_{a})}\right|^{2}\,dz.

Lemma 3.4
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and let
Lemma

3.3
hold with the
Item

p1.
in force. Then there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
such that the following estimate,

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\leq c\lambda^{p}

holds whenever
{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

We will be estimating the terms on the right hand side of
Lemma

3.3
. For any
2\varrho\leq\rho_{1}<\rho_{2}\leq 4\varrho,
from the energy estimate in
Lemma

3.3
we have

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{1}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{1}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{1}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{1}}\right|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{c\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left(\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{p}+a(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}+b(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}\right)\,dz}_{I}

(3.27)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{c\lambda^{p-2}\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}\,dz}_{II}.

Estimate for I:
Applying
(i)
of
Lemma

3.2
with
\theta=1
and
p2
of
Item

p2.
, we have

(3.28)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{p}dz\lesssim\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\lambda^{p}.

Now we estimate

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}a(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}|a(z)-\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}a(w)|\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}dz

\displaystyle+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}a(w)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}dz.

Note that the second term of the above inequality can be estimated by using
(ii)
of
Lemma

3.2
with
\theta=1
. Indeed,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}a(w)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}dz+\lambda^{q-p}a(z_{0})\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}dz

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-p)q}\lambda^{(p-s)\frac{q}{s}}a(z_{0})\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(z)|\nabla u|^{s}\,dz\right)^{\frac{q(s-1)}{s}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}

(3.29)

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{assmp1a}}{\lesssim}\lambda^{p}.

Next, we note that

(3.30)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}|a(z)-\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}a(w)|\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}dz\lesssim\lambda^{p-q}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}dz.

Now we use
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=q,\xi=p,r=2
and
\vartheta=\frac{p}{q}.

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{p}+|\nabla u|^{p}dz\right)\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{q-p}{2}}

(3.31)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{EQUATION3.20}}{\lesssim}\lambda^{p}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{q-p}{2}}.

In a similar way, we estimate

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}b(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}|b(z)-\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}b(w)|\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}dz

\displaystyle+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}b(w)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}dz.

To estimate the second term of the above inequality, we apply
(iii)
of
Lemma

3.2
with
\theta=1.
Indeed,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}b(w)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}b(z)|\nabla u|^{s}dz+\lambda^{s-p}b(z_{0})\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}b(z)|\nabla u|^{s}dz

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-p)q}\lambda^{(p-q)\frac{s}{q}}b(z_{0})\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s(q-1)}{q}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}

(3.32)

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{assmp1a}}{\lesssim}\lambda^{p}.

We also have,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}|b(z)-\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}b(w)|\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}dz\leq c\lambda^{p-s}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}\,dz.

Again, we use
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=s,\xi=p,r=2
and
\vartheta=\frac{p}{s}
to get

(3.33)
