\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}dz\lesssim\lambda^{p}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{s-p}{2}}.

Hence combining the estimates (
3.28
), (
3.29
), (
3.30
), (
3.31
), (
3.32
) and (
3.33
), we get

\displaystyle I\lesssim\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\Bigg(\lambda^{p}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2p-q}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{q-p}{2}}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2p-s}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{s-p}{2}}\Bigg).

Estimate for II:
We shall make use of
Lemma

2.1
with
s=p,\sigma=2,\vartheta=\frac{1}{2}
and
r=2.
It is easy to see

\displaystyle-\frac{n}{2}<\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\frac{n}{p}\right)-\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\right)\frac{n}{2}\,\,\,\,\text{if and only if}\,\,\,\,p>\frac{2n}{n+2}.

This gives

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{p-2}\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\fint_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left(\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{p}+|\nabla u|^{p}\right)dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}dt

\displaystyle\times\left(\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dt

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{p-2}\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{p}dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Using
(i)
of
Lemma

3.2
and
p2
of
Item

p2.
we further have

\displaystyle II\lesssim\lambda^{p-2}\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\lambda\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Therefore combining the above estimates and applying Young’s inequality, from (
3.27
) we get

\displaystyle\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{1}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{1}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{1}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{1}}\right|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\lambda^{2}+c\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\lambda\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+c\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\lambda^{p-q+2}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{q-p}{2}}

\displaystyle+c\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\lambda^{p-s+2}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{s-p}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,p}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)+c\Bigg[\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{q}+\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{4}+\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{\frac{2s}{p-q+2}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{\frac{2s}{p-s+2}}\Bigg]\lambda^{2}.

Finally, using
Lemma

2.2
, we get the desired estimate on
2\varrho.

∎

In the next lemma, we estimate the first term on the right-hand side of
Lemma

3.3
.

Lemma 3.5
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and
Lemma

3.3
holds with the
Item

p1.
in force. Then there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
, depending only on
n,p,q,s
such that for any
\theta,\varepsilon\in(\theta_{0},1)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\right)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{\theta p}+a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}+b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}

whenever
{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{4\varrho}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

Let us first estimate the first term of
H(z,\cdot)
on the left hand side. We shall make use of
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=p,\xi=\theta p,\vartheta=\theta
and
r=2.
It is easy to see that for
\theta\geq\frac{n}{n+2},
the hypothesis of the
Lemma

2.1
satisfied. Indeed,

\displaystyle-\frac{n}{p}\leq\theta\left(1-\frac{n}{\theta p}\right)-(1-\theta)\frac{n}{2}\,\,\,\text{if and only if}\,\,\,\theta\geq\frac{n}{n+2}.

Therefore an application of
Lemma

2.1
gives,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{p}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta p}+|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)p}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{p_intrinsic poincare 2}}{\lesssim}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}\right)\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)p}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{LEMMA7.5}}{\lesssim}\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}\right).

Thus, finally we get

(3.34)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{p}dz\lesssim\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

The second term of
H(z,\cdot)
on the left hand side can be written as

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz}_{I}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}c[a]_{\alpha}\max\left\{\left(\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho\right)^{\alpha},\left(\lambda^{\frac{2\mu-p}{2}}2\varrho\right)^{\alpha}\right\}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz}_{II}.

Estimate for I:
To get an estimate of
I,
we use
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=q,\xi=\theta q,\vartheta=\theta
and
r=2.
One can easily check that the hypothesis of
Lemma

2.1
is satisfied. Indeed,

\displaystyle-\frac{n}{q}\leq\theta\left(1-\frac{n}{\theta q}\right)-(1-\theta)\frac{n}{2}\,\,\,\,\,\text{if and only if}\,\,\,\,\theta\geq\frac{n}{2+n}.

Now
Lemma

2.1
gives

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta q}+\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\right)dz

\displaystyle\times\inf_{z\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)^{1-\theta}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)q}{2}}.

Now to estimate the first term above, we use
Lemma

3.2

(ii)
to get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta q}\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(q-p)\theta}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a^{\theta}(z)|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz

\displaystyle+c\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-s)\frac{\theta q}{s}}\inf_{z\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{z\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)^{\frac{q(s-1)}{s}}

(3.35)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}\lambda^{\theta q}.

Therefore using
p1
and
p2
of
Item

p1.
in (

), we obtain

(3.36)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{\theta}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta q}\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a^{\theta}(z)|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}.

The third term above can be estimated using
Lemma

3.4
and
Item

p1.
as

(3.37)

\displaystyle\inf_{z\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)^{1-\theta}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)q}{2}}\leq c\lambda^{(p-q)(1-\theta)}\lambda^{(1-\theta)q}=\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}.

Combining the estimates (
3.36
)-(
3.37
), we obtain

\displaystyle I=

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz

(3.38)

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left[\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right]+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

Estimate for II:
As previous, using the definition of
\rho_{a}
and
\rho_{b},
we have

\displaystyle[a]_{\alpha}\max\left\{\lambda^{\alpha(-1+\mu)}(2\varrho)^{\alpha},\left(\lambda^{\frac{2\mu-p}{2}}2\varrho\right)^{\alpha}\right\}\leq c\lambda^{p-q}.

Now we use
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=q,\xi=\theta p,\vartheta=\frac{\theta p}{q}
and
r=2.
Note that

\displaystyle-\frac{n}{q}\leq\frac{\theta p}{q}\left(1-\frac{n}{\theta p}\right)-\left(1-\frac{\theta p}{q}\right)\frac{n}{2}\,\,\,\,\,\text{if and only if}\,\,\,\,\,\theta\geq\frac{2p}{(n+2)q}.

Therefore, we find

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{p}dz

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{g_n}}{\lesssim}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta p}+|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{q-\theta p}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{p_intrinsic poincare 2}\,\,\text{and}\,\,\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{LEMMA7.5}}{\lesssim}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}\right)\lambda^{q-\theta p}.

Thus finally, we get

\displaystyle II=

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}c[a]_{\alpha}\max\left\{\left(\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho\right)^{\alpha},\left(\lambda^{\frac{2\mu-p}{2}}2\varrho\right)^{\alpha}\right\}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz

(3.39)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{p-q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}\right)\lambda^{q-\theta p}=\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

Similar analysis can be carried out for the third term in
H(z,\cdot)
on the left hand side. Indeed,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{s}dz

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(w)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{s}dz}_{III}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}c[b]_{\beta}\max\left\{\left(\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho\right)^{\beta},\left(\lambda^{\frac{2\mu-p}{2}}2\varrho\right)^{\beta}\right\}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{s}dz}_{IV}.

Again, using
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=s,\xi=\theta s,\vartheta=\theta
and
r=2
and
Lemma

3.1

(iii)
, we can estimate

(3.40)

\displaystyle III\leq c\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left[\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz\right]+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}

On the other hand, using

\displaystyle[b]_{\beta}\max\left\{\lambda^{\alpha(-1+\mu)}(2\varrho)^{\beta},\left(\lambda^{\frac{2\mu-p}{2}}2\varrho\right)^{\beta}\right\}\leq c\lambda^{p-s}

and
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=s,\xi=\theta p,\vartheta=\frac{\theta p}{s}
and
r=2,
we obtain

(3.41)

\displaystyle IV\lesssim\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

Combining the estimates (
3.34
), (
3.38
), (
3.39
), (
3.40
) and (
3.41
), we conclude the lemma.
∎

Lemma 3.6
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and let
Lemma

3.3
hold with the
Item

p1.
in force. Then there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
depending on
n,p,q
such that for any
\theta,\varepsilon\in(\theta_{0},1),
we have

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dz\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

whenever
{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{4\varrho}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

First we use
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=2,\xi=\theta p,\vartheta=\frac{\theta p}{2}
and
r=2
and an application
Lemma

3.2
gives

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}(2\varrho)}\right|^{2}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}\right)\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{2-\theta p}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{LEMMA7.5}}{\lesssim}\lambda^{2-\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{Q^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}\right)

\displaystyle=\lambda^{2-\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{Q^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{p(1-\theta)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left[\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{Q^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\right]+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

We used Young’s inequality with
(\frac{1}{1-\theta},\frac{1}{\theta})
to obtain the last estimate. This completes the proof.
∎

Now we are ready to prove the reverse Hölder inequality for
p
-phase.

Lemma 3.7
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Moreover, we assume that the energy estimate given in
Lemma

3.3
and the
Item

p1.
on the
p
-intrinsic cylinders hold. Then there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
depending on
n,p,q
such that for any
\theta\in(\theta_{0},1),
we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,|\nabla u|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

whenever
{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{4\varrho}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

From the energy estimate
Lemma

3.3
and the definition of
\rho_{a},\rho_{b},
we have

(3.42)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\right)\,dz+\lambda^{p-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dz.

From
Lemma

3.5
, we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\Bigg(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\Bigg)\,dz\lesssim\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{\theta p}+a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}+b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)\,dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{Young's inequality}}{\leq}\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{\theta p}+a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{p}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s}\right)^{\theta}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}.

On the other hand, from
Lemma

3.6
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{p}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s}\right)^{\theta}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}.

Substituting the above estimates in (
3.42
), we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)\,dz\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)^{\theta}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}.

The proof can be completed by absorbing
\varepsilon\lambda^{p}
in the left-hand side, which is allowed because of
p3
of
Item

p3.
.
∎

3.2.
Reverse Hölder inequality for
(p,q)
-phase

Now we prove reverse Hölder inequality for
(p,q)
-phase. We start by defining the
(p,q)
-intrinsic cylinders.

Assumption 3.2
.

In the
(p,q)
-phase, we assume that the following is satisfied:

pq1.

K\lambda^{p}<a(z_{0})\lambda^{q},\,\,\,K\lambda^{p}\geq b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}.

pq2.

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|^{p}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s})dz<\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\,\,\,\text{holds for all}\,\,\,\rho\in(\rho_{a},\rho_{b}].

pq3.

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{a}}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|^{p}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s})dz=\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}.

pq4.

\frac{a(z_{0})}{2}\leq a(z)\leq 2a(z_{0})\,\,\,\text{holds for every}\,\,\,z\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0}).

The proof of the following lemma goes similarly as
Lemma

3.1
and
Lemma

3.2
. We provide detailed proof of the lemma as these estimates hold for the
(p,s)
and
(p,q,s)
-phases also.

Lemma 3.8
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and the
Item

pq1.
is in force. Then for any
\theta\in\left(\max\left\{\frac{s-1}{p},\frac{1}{p}\right\},1\right]
and
\varepsilon\in(0,1),
there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
such that the following estimates

(i)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta p}dz\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta p}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p},

(ii)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta q}dz\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta q}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta q},

(iii)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta s}dz\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\nabla u\right|^{\theta s}dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta s},

hold whenever
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

Using
Lemma

2.4
with
m=p
and
(p,q)
-intrinsic cylinders, we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta p}\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz+\underbrace{\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\tilde{H}(z,\nabla u)\,dz\right)^{\theta p}}_{I}.

Case
p\geq 2
:
The first term in
I
can be estimated as

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p-1}dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{p-2}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right).

Next, using
pq2
of
Item

pq2.
we note that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)^{p-2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{H\"{o}lder's inequality}}{\leq}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}dz\right)^{(p-2)\theta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)^{(p-2)\theta}\leq 1.

Now using
pq4
of
Item

pq4.
in the second term of
I,
we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)|\nabla u|^{q-1}dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}a(z_{0})}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q-1}dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}dz\right)^{\frac{(q-2)\theta p}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\lambda^{(q-2)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right).

Note that in the last inequality above, we used

(3.43)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}dz\lesssim\lambda^{q}

which can be deduced from
pq2
and
pq4
of
Item

pq2.
.
To estimate the third term in
I,
we use the estimate done in
Step 1
of
Lemma

3.1
. In particular, we using (
3.7
), we get

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(z)|\nabla u|^{s-1}\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle+c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{s-1}

Note that the first term of the above inequality can be estimated as previous and we get

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{p-1}\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)

The estimation of the second term is also similar. Indeed,

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{s-1}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}\,dz\right)^{(s-2)\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-p)\theta p}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{\theta p(s-2)}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{q-bound}}{\leq}\lambda^{(2-p)\theta p}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta p}\lambda^{(s-2)\theta p}\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right).

Therefore, combining the estimates above we obtain

\displaystyle I\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right).

Hence, in this case, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta p}dz\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right).

:
Note that

can also be estimated as

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p-1}dz+\frac{\lambda^{2}a(z_{0})}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q-1}dz

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(z)|\nabla u|^{s-1}\,dz\Bigg)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{EQQQ3.7}}{\lesssim}\lambda^{(2-p)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p-1}dz\right)^{\theta p}+\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q-1}dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-s)\theta p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{s-1}\right)^{\theta p}.

Now as in

3.2
, we can use Young’s inequality with
\left(\frac{1}{2-p},\frac{1}{p-1}\right),

\left(\frac{1}{2-s},\frac{1}{s-1}\right)
to get the desired estimate

. Note that, in the above estimate, we assumed
\frac{2n}{n+2}<p\leq q\leq s\leq 2.
The other possibilities, for instance,

or

can be handled as in the case
p\geq 2.

Now we shall prove

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}\right|^{\theta q}\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz+c\underbrace{\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\tilde{H}(z,\nabla u)\right)^{\theta q}}_{I}.

We estimate

from the above inequality by using (

) and
pq4

.

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{p-1}+\lambda^{(2-q)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-1}

(3.44)

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{p-1}

Now we estimate the third term in (

).

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{s-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}\,dz\right)^{\theta(s-2)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{q-bound}}{\lesssim}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta q}\lambda^{\theta q(s-2)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)

It remains to estimate the fourth term of (
3.44
). Although the the estimate is similar, we present it here.

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{(p-1)q}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{\theta q(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\right)^{\frac{q}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{\frac{\theta q(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{\frac{2}{p}}}\right)^{\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right).

\displaystyle I\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right).
