\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{p-1}+\lambda^{(2-q)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-1}

Case

:
We will estimate each of the above four terms carefully. Let us start with the first term.

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta s}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}\right)^{\frac{\theta s(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s}{p}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{assmpq1b}}{\leq}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{\frac{\theta s(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)

Now we estimate the second term.

\displaystyle\left(\frac{2\lambda^{2}a(z_{0})}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta s}

The estimate on the third term similar and rather easy.

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s(p-1)}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{\theta s(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s}{p}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{assmpq1b}}{\leq}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{\frac{\theta s(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right).

We estimate the fourth term below.

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{s-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta s}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta s}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{\theta s(s-2)}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s}{q}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{q-bound}}{\leq}\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\lambda^{(p-s)\theta s}\lambda^{(s-2)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)

Therefore, combining all the estimates above, we conclude for
p\geq 2
case that,

\displaystyle I\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right).

Case
p<2:
We notice that, using Hölder’s inequality and
\lambda^{p}<\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q},

I
can be estimates as

\displaystyle I\leq 2\lambda^{(2-p)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-q)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)^{q-1}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{(2-s)\theta s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)^{s-1}.

Now using the young’s inequality with the pairs
\left(\frac{1}{2-p},\frac{1}{p-1}\right),\left(\frac{1}{2-q},\frac{1}{q-1}\right),\left(\frac{1}{2-s},\frac{1}{s-1}\right),
we obtain

\displaystyle I\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta s}.

This completes the proof.
∎

\vartheta=\frac{p}{s}.

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|b(z)-\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(w)|\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}[b]_{\beta}\max\left\{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2},\frac{\lambda^{\mu}\rho_{2}}{(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{1/2}}\right\}^{\beta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}\,dz

\displaystyle c\leq\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}[b]_{\beta}\max\left\{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2},\lambda^{\frac{2\mu-p}{2}}\rho_{2}\right\}^{\beta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{s}\,dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\lambda^{p-s}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}\left(\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{p}+|\nabla u|^{p}\right)\,dz\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s-p}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\lambda^{p-s}(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s-p}{2}}.

Hence, combining all the estimates above, we get

\displaystyle I\lesssim\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\left((\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})+\lambda^{p-s}(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}\,dz\right)^{\frac{s-p}{2}}\right)

Estimate for II:
Note that combined with
Lemma

3.4
, it is enough to get an estimate for

\displaystyle II_{q}=\lambda^{q-2}\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}\,dz.

We shall make use of
Lemma

2.1
with
s=q,\sigma=2,\vartheta=\frac{1}{2}
and
r=2.
It is easy to see

\displaystyle-\frac{n}{2}<\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\frac{n}{q}\right)-\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\right)\frac{n}{2}\,\,\,\,\text{if and only if}\,\,\,\,q>\frac{2n}{n+2}.

This gives

\displaystyle II_{q}

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{q-2}\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\fint_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left(\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}+|\nabla u|^{q}\right)dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dt

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{q-2}\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{q}dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Note that from
pq4
of
Item

pq1.
, we have
\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\leq ca(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\leq c\lambda^{q}.
Hence using this combined with
Lemma

3.8
, we further have

\displaystyle II\lesssim(\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2})\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\lambda\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Therefore combining the above estimates and applying Young’s inequality, from (
3.45
), we get

\displaystyle\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{1}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{1}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{1}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{1}}\right|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\lambda^{2}+c\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{2}\lambda\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}\lambda^{2+p-s}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{s-p}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{2}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)+c\left[\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{s}+\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{4}+\left(\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{2}-\rho_{1}}\right)^{\frac{2s}{p-s+2}}\right]\lambda^{2}.

Finally using
Lemma

2.2
, we get the desired estimate on
2\varrho.
Thus we obtained

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\lesssim\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}.

Hence combined with
Lemma

3.4
, we conclude the proof.
∎

Lemma 3.11
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and let
Lemma

3.9
hold with the
Item

pq1.
in force. Then there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
such that for any
\theta,\varepsilon\in\left(\theta_{0},1\right)
for some
\theta_{0}\in(0,1),
we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\right)dz\leq c\left(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)^{1-\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)^{\theta}\,dz+\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})

whenever
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{4\varrho}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

The proof is similar to the
p
-intrinsic case and here we also have the advantage of the bound
a(z)\leq 2a(z_{0}).
We shall proceed as in
Lemma

3.5
. We will make use of
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=p,\xi=\theta p,\vartheta=\theta,r=2
to estimate the first term in the left hand side. First, we note that

\displaystyle-\frac{n}{p}\leq\theta\left(1-\frac{n}{\theta p}\right)-(1-\theta)\frac{n}{2}\,\,\,\,\text{if and only if}\,\,\,\,\theta\geq\frac{n}{n+2}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{p}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta p}dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}dz\right)\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)p}{2}}.

Now using
Lemma

3.8
and
Lemma

3.10
in the above inequality, we find

(3.46)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{p}\,dz\leq\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

Similarly using
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=q,\xi=\theta q,\vartheta=\theta,r=2
we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz

\displaystyle\leq 2a(z_{0})\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z_{0})^{\theta}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta q}dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z_{0})^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}a(z_{0})^{1-\theta}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)q}{2}}.

In the first inequality above, we used
pq4
of
Item

pq4.
. Now using
Lemma

3.8
and
Lemma

3.10
in the above inequality, we find

(3.47)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz\leq c(a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{1-\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a^{\theta}(z_{0})|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz+\varepsilon a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}.

It remains to estimate the third term in the left hand side.

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{s}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}b(w)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{s}\,dz}_{I}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{c[b]_{\beta}\max\left\{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho,\frac{\lambda^{\mu}2\varrho}{(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{1/2}}\right\}^{\beta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{s}\,dz}_{II}

We estimate
I
by using
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=s,\xi=\theta s,\vartheta=\theta,r=2.

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}b(w)^{\theta}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta s}\,dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz

\displaystyle\times\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}b(w)^{1-\theta}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{2\varrho}}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)s}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{p_intrinsic poincare 2},\ref{3_lem7.10}\,\,\text{and}\,\,\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{LEMMA7.11}}{\lesssim}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}b(w)^{1-\theta}\lambda^{(1-\theta)s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}b(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz+\varepsilon\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}b(w)^{\theta}\lambda^{\theta s}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{(p-s)(1-\theta)}\lambda^{(1-\theta)s}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz+\varepsilon\inf_{w\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}b(w)\lambda^{s}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{p(1-\theta)}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

We use

(3.48)

\displaystyle\max\left\{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho,\frac{\lambda^{\mu}2\varrho}{(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{1/2}}\right\}^{\beta}\leq\max\left\{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho,\lambda^{\frac{2\mu-p}{2}}2\varrho\right\}^{\beta}\leq\frac{K}{2[b]_{\beta}}\lambda^{p-s}

and
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=s,\xi=\theta p,\vartheta=\frac{\theta p}{s},r=2.
Note that the inequality condition on the
Lemma

2.1
is satisfied with this choice, since

\displaystyle\frac{ns}{(n+2)p}\leq\frac{n}{n+2}\left(1+\frac{2}{(n+2)p}\right)\leq\frac{n}{n+2}\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\right)=\frac{n+1}{n+2}<1,

where we used
s\leq p+\frac{2}{n+2}
and
p>\frac{2n}{n+2}.

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{p-s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta p}+|\nabla u|^{\theta p},dz\right)\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{2\varrho}}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{s-\theta p}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{p_intrinsic poincare 2},\ref{1_lem7.10}\,\,\text{and}\,\,\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{LEMMA7.11}}{\lesssim}\lambda^{p-s}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}\right)\lambda^{s-\theta p}

Thus combining the above estimates, we obtain

(3.49)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}b(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{s}\,dz\lesssim\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

Hence combining (
3.46
),(
3.47
) and (
3.49
) with
pq4
of
Item

pq4.
, we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}+\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)^{1-\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{\theta p}+a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}+b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)dz+\varepsilon\left(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right).

Since
\theta<1
and
1-\theta<1
we can further estimate the right hand side of the above inequality and get the claim by using

|a+b|^{\beta}\leq(1+\delta)^{\beta-1}|a|^{\beta}+\left(1+\frac{1}{\delta}\right)^{\beta-1}|b|^{\beta}

for
1\leq\beta<\infty
and arbitrary
a,b\in\mathds{R}
and
\delta>0.
This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 3.12
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and let
Lemma

3.9
hold with
Item

pq1.
in force. Then there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
depending on
n,p,q
such that for any
\theta,\varepsilon\in(\theta_{0},1),
we have

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\left[\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}+a^{\theta}(z)|\nabla u|^{\theta q}+b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\right]

whenever
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{4\varrho}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

Following the proof of
Lemma

3.6
we have

(3.50)

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dz\lesssim\left[\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{G^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\right].

We shall apply
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=2,\xi=\theta q,\vartheta=\frac{\theta q}{2}
and
r=2.
It can be easily checked that

\displaystyle-\frac{n}{2}\leq\frac{\theta q}{2}\left(1-\frac{n}{\theta q}\right)-\left(1-\frac{\theta q}{2}\right)\frac{n}{2}\,\,\,\,\text{if and only if}\,\,\,\,\theta\geq\frac{n}{(n+2)q}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta q}dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q)}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{2-\theta q}{2}}.

Moreover, applying
Lemma

3.8
and
Lemma

3.10
we find

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dz\leq c\lambda^{2-\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{2}.

Hence

\displaystyle a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}(2\varrho)}\right|^{2}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim a(z_{0})\lambda^{(1-\theta)q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)+\varepsilon a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}

\displaystyle=c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)^{1-\theta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}a(z_{0})^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)+\varepsilon a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}.

Now using Young’s inequality we get

(3.51)

\displaystyle a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dz\lesssim\left[\varepsilon a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}a(z_{0})^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\right].

Therefore, adding (
3.50
) and (
3.51
), we get the desired result.
∎

Now we prove the reverse Hölder inequality in the
(p,q)
-intrinsic case.

Lemma 3.13
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Moreover, we assume that the energy estimate given in
Lemma

3.9
and
Item

pq1.
hold for
(p,q)
-intrinsic cylinders. Then there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
such that for any
\theta\in(\theta_{0},1),
we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,\nabla u)\,dz\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,\nabla u)^{\theta}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}.

Proof.

From the energy estimate
Lemma

3.9
and the definition of
\rho_{a},\rho_{b},
we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,\nabla u)dz\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\right)\,dz

(3.52)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dz.

From
Lemma

3.11
, we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\Bigg(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\Bigg)\,dz

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{1-\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)^{\theta}dz+\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{Young's inequality}}{\leq}\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)^{\theta}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}.

Moreover, from
Lemma

3.12
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,\nabla u)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}.

Substituting the above estimates in (
3.52
), we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)dz\leq\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,\nabla u)^{\theta}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}.

The proof can be completed by absorbing
\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})
on the left-hand side, which is allowed due to
pq3
of
Item

pq3.
.
∎

3.3.
Reverse Hölder inequality for
(p,s)
-phase

In this subsection, we prove the reverse Hölder inequality for
(p,s)
-phase.

Assumption 3.3
.

In the
(p,s)
-phase, we assume that the following is satisfied:

ps1.

K\lambda^{p}\geq a(z_{0})\lambda^{q},\,\,\,K\lambda^{p}<b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}.

ps2.

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|^{p}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s})dz<\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}\,\,\,\text{holds for all}\,\,\,\rho\in(\rho_{a},\rho_{b}].

ps3.

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|^{p}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s})dz=\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}.

ps4.

\frac{b(z_{0})}{2}\leq b(z)\leq 2b(z_{0})\,\,\,\text{holds for every}\,\,\,z\in\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0}).

We shall start with parabolic Sobolev-Poincaré inequalities for
(p,s)
-phase.

(3.53)

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}a(w)^{\theta}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta q}\,dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\times\inf_{w\in\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{1-\theta}\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,s)}_{2\varrho}}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)q}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{p_intrinsic poincare 2},\ref{3_lem7.10}\,\,\text{and}\,\,\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{LEMMA3.16}}{\lesssim}\inf_{w\in\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(w)^{1-\theta}\lambda^{(1-\theta)q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\inf_{w\in\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}a(w)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz+\varepsilon\inf_{w\in\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}a(w)^{\theta}\lambda^{\theta q}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{(p-q)(1-\theta)}\lambda^{(1-\theta)q}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz+\varepsilon\inf_{w\in\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}a(w)\lambda^{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{p(1-\theta)}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

We again use

\displaystyle\max\left\{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho,\frac{\lambda^{\mu}2\varrho}{(\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s})^{1/2}}\right\}^{\alpha}\leq\max\left\{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho,\lambda^{\frac{2\mu-p}{2}}2\varrho\right\}^{\alpha}\lesssim\lambda^{p-q}

and
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=q,\xi=\theta p,\vartheta=\frac{\theta p}{q},r=2.
Note that the inequality condition on the
Lemma

2.1
is satisfied with this choice, since

\displaystyle\frac{nq}{(n+2)p}\leq\frac{n}{n+2}\left(1+\frac{2}{(n+2)p}\right)\leq\frac{n}{n+2}\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\right)=\frac{n+1}{n+2}<1,

where we used
q\leq p+\frac{2}{n+2}
and
p>\frac{2n}{n+2}.

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{p-q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{\theta p}+|\nabla u|^{\theta p},dz\right)\left(\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,s)}_{2\varrho}}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{q-\theta p}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{p_intrinsic poincare 2},\ref{1_lem7.10}\,\,\text{and}\,\,\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{LEMMA3.16}}{\lesssim}\lambda^{p-q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz+\varepsilon\lambda^{\theta p}\right)\lambda^{q-\theta p}

Thus combining the above estimates, we obtain

(3.55)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}a(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}\,dz\lesssim\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

Hence combining (
3.53
),(
3.54
) and (
3.55
) with
ps4
of
Item

ps4.
, we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}+\left(b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)^{1-\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{\theta p}+a(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}+b(z)^{\theta}|\nabla u|^{\theta s}\right)dz+\varepsilon\left(\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right).

This completes the proof.
∎

(3.56)

\displaystyle+(\lambda^{p-2}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s-2})\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}\,dz.

3.4.
Reverse Hölder inequality for
(p,q,s)
-phase

In this subsection, we prove the reverse Hölder inequality for

-phase.

Assumption 3.4
.

In the
(p,q,s)
-phase, we assume that the following is satisfied:

pqs1.

K\lambda^{p}<a(z_{0})\lambda^{q},\,\,\,K\lambda^{p}<b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}.

pqs2.

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|^{p}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s})dz<\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}\,\,\,\text{holds for all}\,\,\,\rho\in(\rho_{a},\rho_{b}].

pqs3.

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|^{p}+a(z)|\nabla u|^{q}+b(z)|\nabla u|^{s})dz=\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}.

pqs4.

\frac{b(z_{0})}{2}\leq b(z)\leq 2b(z_{0})
and
\frac{b(z_{0})}{2}\leq b(z)\leq 2b(z_{0})\,\,\,\text{holds for every}\,\,\,z\in\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0}).

We shall start with parabolic Sobolev-Poincaré inequalities for

-phases.

) for the

Cut-off functions for
(p,q,s)
-phase.

\displaystyle\eta:=\eta(x)\in C_{c}^{\infty}(B_{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{b}}(x_{0})),\quad\eta\equiv 1\,\,\text{on}\,B_{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{a}}(x_{0}),\quad 0\leq\eta\leq 1\,\,\,\,\,\text{and}\,\,\,\,\,\,|\nabla\eta|\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}(\rho_{b}-\rho_{a})}

\displaystyle\zeta\in C^{\infty}_{c}\left(t_{0}-\frac{\lambda^{2\mu}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}}(\rho_{b}-h_{0})^{2},t_{0}+\frac{\lambda^{2\mu}}{\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}}(\rho_{b}-h_{0})^{2}\right),\,\,\,\,\,0\leq\zeta\leq 1,

Then the energy estimate for the
