-phase reads is as follows.

Lemma 3.21
.

Let
u
be a weak solution of (
1.1
). Then we have the following energy estimate:

\displaystyle(\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s-2})\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,q,s)}_{\rho_{a}}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{\rho_{a}}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{a}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}\rho_{a}}\right|^{2}\,dx+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{a}}(z_{0})}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)\,dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}(\rho_{b}-\rho_{a})}\right|\right)\,dz+(\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s-2})\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}(\rho_{b}-\rho_{a})}\right|^{2}\,dz.

We omit the proof of the next lemma as it can be completed using similar arguments as previous.

Lemma 3.22
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and let
Lemma

3.21
hold with the
Item

pqs1.
in force. Then there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
such that the following estimate

\displaystyle\left(\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s-2}\right)\sup_{I^{\lambda,(p,s)}_{2\varrho}(t_{0})}\fint_{B^{\lambda}_{2\rho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{2\rho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dx\leq c\left(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}\right)

holds whenever
\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Now we prove the following lemma, which leads us to reverse Hölder inequality in this case.

Lemma 3.23
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and let
Lemma

3.21
hold with the
Item

pqs1.
in force. Then there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
such that for any
\theta,\varepsilon\in\left(\theta_{0},1\right)
for some
\theta_{0}\in(0,1),
we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}\right)^{1-\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)^{\theta}\,dz

\displaystyle+\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{s})

whenever
\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{4\varrho}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

The first term on the right hand side of the statement is estimated as

(3.57)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{p}\,dz\leq\lambda^{(1-\theta)p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)+\varepsilon\lambda^{p}.

Using
pqs4
and
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=q,\xi=\theta q,\vartheta=\theta,r=2
, we get an estimate on the second term.

(3.58)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{q}dz\leq c(a(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{1-\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}a^{\theta}(z_{0})|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz+\varepsilon a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}.

Lastly, using
pqs4
and
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=s,\xi=\theta s,\vartheta=\theta,r=2,
we estimate the third term.

(3.59)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b(z)\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{s}dz\leq c(b(z_{0})\lambda^{s})^{1-\theta}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}b^{\theta}(z_{0})|\nabla u|^{\theta s}dz+\varepsilon b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}.

Combining the estimates (
3.57
), (
3.58
) and (
3.59
), we conclude the proof.
∎

Since the proofs of next two lemmas will be similar as previous, we only state them below.

Lemma 3.24
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
) and
Lemma

3.21
holds with the
Item

pqs1.
is in force. Then there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
depending on
n,p,q
such that for any
\theta,\varepsilon\in(\theta_{0},1),
we have

\displaystyle(\lambda^{p-2}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s-2})

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-(u)_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}}}{\lambda^{-1+\mu}2\varrho}\right|^{2}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\left[\varepsilon(\lambda^{p}+a(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s})+c(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,|\nabla u|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\right]

whenever
\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{4\varrho}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Applying the above
Lemma

3.22
,
Lemma

3.23
and
Lemma

3.24
, we deduce the reverse Hölder inequality for
(p,q,s)
-phase.

Lemma 3.25
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Moreover, we assume that the energy estimate given in
Lemma

3.21
and
Item

pqs1.
hold for
(p,q,s)
-intrinsic cylinders. Then there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
such that for any
\theta\in(\theta_{0},1),
we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,\nabla u)\,dz\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.75554pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.13002pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.08461pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-8.33461pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{2\varrho}(z_{0})}H(z,\nabla u)^{\theta}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}.

4.
Gradient higher integrability

In this section, we prove the gradient higher integrability for the solutions to (
1.1
). In particular, we prove
Theorem

1.1
. Let us recall the notations
(iv)
,
Section

1.2.1
i.e.,

\frac{n}{n+2}<\mu\leq\min\left\{1,\frac{p}{2}\right\},

and

(vi)
,
Section

1.2.1
with
2r,
i.e.,

{\mathcal{P}}_{2r}(z_{0}):=B_{2r}(x_{0})\times(t_{0}-4r^{2},t_{0}+4r^{2}).

4.1.
Existence of intrinsic cylinders on superlevel sets

We first show the existence of
p
-intrinsic and
(p,q)
-intrinsic cylinders on the superlevel sets. Let

(4.1)

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}^{(n+2)\mu-n}=\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}_{2r}(z_{0})}H(z,|\nabla u|)dz+1,\,\,\,\text{and}\,\,\,\,\,\,\Lambda_{0}=\lambda_{0}^{p}+\sup_{z\in{\mathcal{P}}_{2r}(z_{0})}a(z)\lambda_{0}^{q}+\sup_{z\in{\mathcal{P}}_{2r}(z_{0})}b(z)\lambda_{0}^{s}.

For
\uprho\in[r,2r],
we define the superlevel sets

(4.2)

\displaystyle E_{\Lambda}=\left\{z\in\Omega_{T}\,\,\Big|\,\,H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)>\Lambda\right\}\,\,\,\,\text{and}\,\,\,\,

\displaystyle E_{\Lambda,\uprho}=E_{\Lambda}\cap{\mathcal{P}}_{\uprho}(z_{0}):=\left\{z\in{\mathcal{P}}_{\uprho}(z_{0})\,\,\Big|\,\,H\left(z,|\nabla u|\right)>\Lambda\right\}.

Let
0<r\leq r_{1}<r_{2}\leq 2r
and

(4.3)

\displaystyle\Lambda>\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{s(n+2)}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\Lambda_{0}\,\,\,\,\,\text{for some}\,\,\,\,\kappa>1.

Let
\lambda_{\tilde{z}}>0
be such that
\Lambda=\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}+b(\tilde{z})\lambda^{s}_{\tilde{z}}
for any
\tilde{z}\in E_{\Lambda,r_{1}}.
We claim that

(4.4)

\displaystyle\lambda_{\tilde{z}}>\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\lambda_{0}.

On the contrary, let us assume

\lambda_{\tilde{z}}\leq\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\lambda_{0}.

Then we find

\displaystyle\Lambda

\displaystyle=\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}+b(\tilde{z})\lambda^{s}_{\tilde{z}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{p(n+2)}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\lambda^{p}_{0}+a(\tilde{z})\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{q(n+2)}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\lambda^{q}_{0}+b(\tilde{z})\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{s(n+2)}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\lambda^{s}_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{s(n+2)}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\left(\lambda^{p}_{0}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{0}+b(\tilde{z})\lambda^{s}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{s(n+2)}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\Lambda_{0},

which is a contradiction to (
4.3
). In the third inequality above, we used that
\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\geq 1,
and
p\leq q\leq s.
Therefore for
\uprho\in\left[\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{2\kappa},r_{2}-r_{1}\right),
we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho}(\tilde{z})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda^{2\mu-p+(\mu-1)n}_{\tilde{z}}\uprho^{n+2}}\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho}(\tilde{z})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{p-2\mu+(1-\mu)n}_{\tilde{z}}\left(\frac{2r}{\uprho}\right)^{n+2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}_{2r}(z_{0})}H(z,|\nabla u|)dz

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n+2}\lambda^{p-2\mu+(1-\mu)n}_{\tilde{z}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}_{2r}(z_{0})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz.

In the last inequality, we used the choice of
\uprho
given above. Further estimating the right hand side, we get

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho}(\tilde{z})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{def_la_0}}{\leq}\left(\frac{4\kappa r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n+2}\lambda^{p-2\mu+(1-\mu)n}_{\tilde{z}}\lambda^{(n+2)\mu-n}_{0}

(4.5)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{EQQ6.2}}{<}\left(\frac{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}{\lambda_{0}}\right)^{(n+2)\mu-n}\lambda^{p-2\mu+(1-\mu)n}_{\tilde{z}}\lambda^{(n+2)\mu-n}_{0}=\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}.

Since
\tilde{z}\in E_{\Lambda,r_{1}}
and
\Lambda=\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}+b(\tilde{z})\lambda^{s}_{\tilde{z}}
for any
\tilde{z}\in E_{\Lambda,r_{1}},
we conclude that
\tilde{z}\in E_{\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}},r_{1}}.
Now for each
\tilde{z}\in E_{\Lambda,r_{1}},
by Lebesgue differentiation theorem we have

(4.6)

\displaystyle\lim_{\uprho\to 0}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho}(\tilde{z})}|H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz=|\nabla u(\tilde{z})|^{p}+a(\tilde{z})|\nabla u(\tilde{z})|^{q}+b(\tilde{z})|\nabla u(\tilde{z})|>\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}.

Now from (
4.5
) and (
4.6
) we conclude that there exists a radius
\uprho_{\tilde{z}}\in\left(0,\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{2\kappa}\right)
such that

(4.7)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho_{\tilde{z}}}(\tilde{z})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz=\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}

and for every
\uprho\in\left(\uprho_{\tilde{z}},\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{2\kappa}\right]
we have

(4.8)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho}(\tilde{z})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz<\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}.

Now proceeding as (
4.5
) and using (
4.7
) we get

\displaystyle\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}=\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho_{\tilde{z}}}(\tilde{z})}H(\tilde{z},|\nabla u|)\,dz

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\uprho^{n+2}_{\tilde{z}}\lambda^{2\mu-p+(\mu-1)n}_{\tilde{z}}}\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho_{\tilde{z}}}(\tilde{z})}H(\tilde{z},|\nabla u|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(2r)^{n+2}}{\uprho^{n+2}_{\tilde{z}}\lambda^{2\mu-p+(\mu-1)n}_{\tilde{z}}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}_{2r}(z_{0})}H(\tilde{z},|\nabla u|)\,dz

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{def_la_0}}{<}\left(\frac{2r}{\uprho_{\tilde{z}}}\right)^{n+2}\lambda^{p-2\mu+(1-\mu)n}_{\tilde{z}}\lambda^{(n+2)\mu-n}_{0}.

This implies

(4.9)

\displaystyle\lambda_{\tilde{z}}<\left(\frac{2r}{\uprho_{\tilde{z}}}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{(n+2)\mu-n}}\lambda_{0}.

(4.15)

\displaystyle a(\tilde{z})<2[a]_{\alpha}\max\left\{(\lambda^{-1+\mu}_{\tilde{z}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}})^{\alpha},\left(\frac{\lambda^{\mu}_{\tilde{z}}}{\sqrt{\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}}\right)^{\alpha}\right\},

(4.16)

(4.17)

\displaystyle b(\tilde{z})<2[b]_{\beta}\max\left\{(\lambda^{-1+\mu}_{\tilde{z}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}})^{\beta},\left(\frac{\lambda^{\mu}_{\tilde{z}}}{\sqrt{\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+b(\tilde{z})\lambda^{s}_{\tilde{z}}}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}}\right)^{\beta}\right\},

(4.18)

\displaystyle a(\tilde{z})\geq 2[a]_{\alpha}\max\left\{(\lambda^{-1+\mu}_{\tilde{z}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}})^{\alpha},\left(\frac{\lambda^{\mu}_{\tilde{z}}}{\sqrt{\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}+b(\tilde{z})\lambda^{s}_{tz}}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}}\right)^{\alpha}\right\},

(4.19)

(4.20)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho}(\tilde{z})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz\leq\frac{\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}}{\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho}(\tilde{z})}H(z,|\nabla u|)\,dz<\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}.

\displaystyle<a(\tilde{z})\leq\inf_{z\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho_{\tilde{z}}}(\tilde{z})}a(z)+[a]_{\alpha}\max\left\{(\lambda^{-1+\mu}_{\tilde{z}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}})^{\alpha},\left(\frac{\lambda^{\mu}_{\tilde{z}}}{\sqrt{\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}}\right)^{\alpha}\right\}.

From the above inequality, we get

\displaystyle[a]_{\alpha}\max\left\{(\lambda^{-1+\mu}_{\tilde{z}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}})^{\alpha},\left(\frac{\lambda^{\mu}_{\tilde{z}}}{\sqrt{\lambda^{p}_{\tilde{z}}+a(\tilde{z})\lambda^{q}_{\tilde{z}}}}\uprho_{\tilde{z}}\right)^{\alpha}\right\}\leq\inf_{z\in{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda_{\tilde{z}}}_{\uprho_{\tilde{z}}}(\tilde{z})}a(z).

Using these we conclude that
