\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}}\right)^{\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{(p-1)q}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}}\right)^{\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{\theta q(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta p}\right)^{\frac{q}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s}}\right)^{\theta q}(\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s})^{\frac{\theta q(p-2)}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{(\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s})^{\frac{2}{p}}}\right)^{\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right).

Therefore, combining all the estimates above, we finally get

\displaystyle I\lesssim\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{{\mathcal{Q}}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right).

Case
p<2
:
We note that, using Hölder’s inequality and
\lambda^{p}<\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{s},
(
A.3
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{p-1}+\lambda^{(2-s)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}dz\right)^{s-1}

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\lambda^{(p-q)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)^{q-1}+\lambda^{(2-p)\theta q}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.4445pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-12.61888pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.59584pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin={c}]{18.0}{$-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.84584pt}}\!\iint_{\mathcal{S}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{\theta q}\,dz\right)^{p-1}.

Now, we use Young’s inequality as in
Lemma

3.2
to get
(ii)
. In the scenario, when
q\geq 2
or
s\geq 2,
we use the estimate of the case
p\geq 2.
This completes the proof of
Eq.

A.1
.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

3.20
.

In this case we have the advantage of having the bounds on
a(z)
and
b(z)
in the intrinsic cylinders, that is,
\frac{a(z_{0})}{2}\leq a(z)\leq a(z_{0})
and
\frac{b(z_{0})}{2}\leq b(z)\leq b(z_{0})
for every
z\in\mathcal{G}^{\lambda}_{\rho_{b}}(z_{0}).
Using these bounds and following similar calculations as in
Lemma

3.8
, we can complete the proof.
∎
